Traits

Blood Frenzy. The dire wolf has Advantage on attack rolls against any creature that doesn't have all its Hit Points.

Pack Tactics. The dire wolf has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the dire wolf's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The dire wolf makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Hamstring Bite.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d10 + 3) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Hamstring Bite. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target's Speed is halved and it can't take Reactions until the end of the dire wolf's next turn.

Trench Hymn Leviathan

Senses blindsight 120 ft.; Passive Perception 19

Languages Common

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The hymn leviathan can breathe air and water.

Fear Aura. The hymn leviathan emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the hymn leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The hymn leviathan makes two Flukes attacks.

Flukes. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Drowning Hymn (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Retributive Shockwave. One creature within 5 feet that hit the hymn leviathan with an attack roll since the end of the hymn leviathan's last turn takes 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the hymn leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Circling Lunge. The hymn leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Flukes attack.

Shouldering Surge. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the hymn leviathan. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The leviathan opens by singing — a subsonic trench-hymn that rolls out through the water and makes hulls and eardrums split before it ever closes. Each round it circles at the edge of reach, hammering with the same drowning song and shouldering through anything that survives, refusing to trade blows face to face. Under pressure it does not flee: it rises, sings louder, and drags the fight down into the lightless trench where its song has the whole ocean for a throat.
[concept] lore: It has lurked in the deepest trench since the first keel broke the surface, singing down through the dark because every hull that sinks into its water is a note it wants to keep.

**Concept:** a mud-caked bog hound that drags fishermen under

```
Bog Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 30 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Drag Under. While the hound is in water and has a creature grappled, at the start of each of its turns, the hound pulls a creature it is grappling feet toward or beneath the water's surface; if the creature ends its turn fully submerged and cannot breathe water, it must succeed on a Constitution saving throw.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the hound's turns. The hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the hound. If the hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The hound makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Opportunist's Lash.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Opportunist's Lash: One creature within 30 feet of the hound that is grappled by it must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 11 (2d10) piercing damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

It fights with a patient, territorial viciousness, refusing to retreat from its muddy ambush.
[concept] lore: Born from the drowned and mud-swallowed hounds of fishermen lost in the marsh, it haunts the shallows and drags the living under to share its waterlogged grave.

Plague Knell

Medium Undead, Neutral

AC 11
Initiative +1 (11)

HP 45 (10d8)

Speed 5 ft., Fly 40 ft. (hover)

MOD
SAVE
STR
7
-2
-2

+0

13
+1
+3

12
+1
+1
MOD
SAVE
CON
10
+0
+0

17
+3
+3

Skills Perception +3, Stealth +5

Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, Slashing, Thunder

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Faultless Tracker. The knell knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.

Ethereal Sight. The knell can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The knell can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The knell makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Lingering Brand.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Lingering Brand. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is branded: it takes 3 (1d6) Necrotic damage at the start of each of its turns until it uses its action to tear the brand away.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the knell and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the knell's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the knell can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the knell; the knell disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The knell now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The knell can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The knell's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the knell leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the knell appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The knell casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The knell is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

[concept] identity: The bell-ringer's ghost drifts through the chapel walls unseen until it chooses its mark, then lets a single heavy toll of its spectral bell fall — a knell that marks the victim as dying and wrings the warmth from them at range. Every round after, it keeps its distance and tolls again and again, each peal draining life toward the one fated to die, and it lingers to watch the standing give way to the kneeling. It cannot bear true light and recoils from it, but it does not flee — it has already heard the hour of its own passing and waits, patient and terrible, to be proven wrong.
[concept] lore: A bell-ringer who died mid-pull, his last peal rung for a plague-victim who outlived him; now he tolls for whomever the bell names, unable to rest until every announced death arrives — and he resents anyone who survives his knell.

[concept] identity: An owlbear that has learned to climb does not lumber out of a burrow — it drops from a tree limb, a rafter, or a cliff face onto the back of the rearmost traveler, bearing them down with hooked foreclaws before the rest of the party can turn around. Every round after that it scrambles up bark, stone, or wall, hangs just above reach, and drops again on whoever is isolated, using the third dimension the way a wolf uses the pack line; it never fights a standing battle if a vertical escape exists. Wounded, it doesn't flee far — it climbs high and waits, screeching down at whatever bleeds below, willing to drop only when prey strays from the group again.
[concept] lore: A hungry owlbear that grew up around a cliffside rookery learned that the herd it feared could be taken one animal at a time from above; now it hunts any road that runs beneath a tree line or ledge, dragging kills up into the branches to eat in peace. Huge ursine body, matted feathers and hooked talons, and eyes that track the same way from a branch as they do from a trailhead.

Hollow Gloam
Huge elemental, neutral
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

210 (20d12 + 80)

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

15 (13,000 XP)

+5

Glacial Aura. At the end of each of the hollow gloam's turns, each creature within 5 feet of the hollow gloam takes 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Cold Absorption. Whenever the hollow gloam is subjected to cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the cold damage dealt.
Siege Monster. The hollow gloam deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The hollow gloam makes two Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Frost Follow-Up.
Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 22 (4d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Killing Cold (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.
Frost Follow-Up: One creature within 30 feet of the hollow gloam that is deafened must make a DC 17 Strength saving throw, taking 54 (12d8) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.

[concept] identity: The glacier's heart opens a fight by simply walking forward, grinding through stone and timber alike while the killing cold in its wake turns the ground to rime and the air to shearing frost; anything that survives the first pass is caught in a widening field of lethality rather than pursued. Each round it advances on the largest obstruction or warmest body it can sense and breaks it, healing hairline fractures in its crystalline core from the ambient heat it drains. It is not cruel, only implacable: it does not toy with prey, will not flee when bloodied, and fights to the shattering of its own heart, because a heart of ice does not know retreat — only the slow work of becoming winter.
[concept] lore: When a glacier dies of a wound deep in its face, the last heat it hoarded in its core sometimes calcifies into a walking heart that remembers only the cold it came from and the long, patient urge to give it back to the valley.

## Vampire — a legendary

**Concept:** name: Vampire | who keeps a garden of thorns

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CHA +9

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Thorn Garden Walker. Difficult terrain composed of briars, hedges, and rooted plants doesn't cost the vampire extra movement, and the vampire can move across briars, hedges, and rooted plants without needing to make an ability check.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite. It can replace one attack with a use of Thorned Roots.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Thorned Roots: One creature within 60 feet must succeed on a DC 17 Dexterity saving throw or be restrained until the start of its next turn.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
Legendary Actions

The vampire can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The vampire regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathless Strike: The vampire moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Grave Strike attack.
Beguile: The vampire casts command, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). The vampire can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

[concept] identity: The Thorn-Gardener waits motionless inside its own hedge, a bloodless undead shape half-lost among the briars, until a warm body crosses the garden's edge — then it erupts from cover in a single strike, driving a long thorn through the victim's chest to root them where they stand. Every round after, it fades back into the thorns and circles, pricking with poisoned spines and harvesting the blood that feeds the garden, always returning to the bush that hides it. Wounded, it does not flee; it withdraws into the deepest tangle of its hedge and fights to the death among its own roots, patient and possessive of every drop it has planted.
[concept] lore: Once a vampire who traded its coffin for a walled garden, it now roots its victims in the soil and waters its thorns with their blood, feeding the hedge that has slowly become its true body.

Swallowing Shale. If the revenant is grappling a creature at the start of its turn, the creature takes 9 (2d8) thunder damage and the revenant can immediately use Entomb on it without spending a bonus action.
Buried in Scree. While the revenant remains motionless, it is indistinguishable from loose scree, rubble, or heaped stone.
Entomb. As a bonus action, the revenant entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the revenant, and takes 7 (2d6) thunder damage at the start of each of the revenant's turns. The revenant can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the revenant takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the revenant must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the revenant. If the revenant dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The revenant makes two Crushing Slide attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Scouring Scree.
Crushing Slide: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) thunder damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Scouring Scree: Each creature in a 30-foot cone must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the end of its next turn.
```

Traits

Molten Seams. A creature that touches the tyrant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the tyrant, the tyrant and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the tyrant doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Slag-Fling attacks.

Slag-Fling. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Bonus Actions

Retaliatory Slag. One creature within 5 feet that hit the tyrant with an attack roll since the end of the tyrant's last turn takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Hold the Line. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Slag-Fling attack.

Rally the Forges. One ally of the tyrant that can see or hear it can immediately use its Reaction to move up to half its Speed or to make one attack.

Quell Dismay. The tyrant and each of its allies within 10 feet of it that can see or hear it end the Charmed and Frightened conditions on themselves.

Pinning Arms. While the scythe horror has a creature Grappled with its Spitting Maw, the scythe horror can pin that creature with two of its arms; the Grappled creature cannot move farther than the scythe horror's reach allows unless it first escapes the grapple.

Caustic Crust. At the end of each of the scythe horror's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the scythe horror takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Scythe Limb. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

Swallow. The scythe horror swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the scythe horror, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the scythe horror's turns. If the scythe horror takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the swallowed creature, the scythe horror must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the scythe horror. If the scythe horror dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

```
Vault Warden
Huge construct, unaligned
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

171 (18d12 + 54)

Speed: 

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

17 (+3)

	

6 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +8, Perception +4

Saving Throws: 

STR +8, CON +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Fire Absorption. Whenever the warden is subjected to fire damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the fire damage dealt.
Immutable Form. The warden can't shape-shift.
Siege Monster. The warden deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The warden makes two Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Punish the Fallen.
Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (2d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 10 (3d6) fire damage.
Iron Shove (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 66 (12d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone.
Punish the Fallen: One creature within 30 feet of the warden that is prone must make a DC 16 Strength saving throw, taking 42 (12d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

[concept] identity: A silent colossus of welded black iron, it advances in a straight line at a slow, unstoppable pace, letting its sheer bulk and plated fists clear the battlefield ahead of it. Each round it hammers the nearest target with a slam that shudders the ground, or sweeps a whole line of foes back with a broad, iron-shouldered charge. Too heavy to run and too duty-bound to retreat, it fights until dismantled, grinding forward even as its plates split and its seams vent sparks.
[concept] lore: Forge-halls and siege-lines pour these black iron sentinels to hold a gate or a vault long after the smiths who cast them are dead, and each one walks its assigned circuit until something breaks it or the charge it was set to keep is finally fulfilled.

[concept] identity: A flock of tarnished funeral bells drifts in on the death-rattle, ringing itself in slow, mournful peals that thicken into a single deafening toll as it settles over the dying; it does not hunt the healthy, only gathering where breath fails and following the wounded until they stop. Round after round it circles its chosen mark, hammering out a ringing force that rattles bone and scatters thought, and if the swarm is split it only redoubles its clamor — it fights to the last bell, never fleeing.
[concept] lore: Forged in a bell-foundry that cast the death-knells of an unburied plague-city, these bells rose from the cooling molds when the last ringer died mid-call, and they still seek the dying to finish the funeral no one survived to give.

## Altar-Gnaw Ooze — bands soft

**Concept:** a cathedral-vault ooze that digests reliquaries

```
Altar-Gnaw Ooze
Large ooze, unaligned
Armor Class

10 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

153 (18d10 + 54)

Speed: 

20 ft., climb 20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

6 (-2)

	

16 (+3)

	

2 (-4)

	

8 (-1)

	

1 (-5)

Damage Immunities: 

acid

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, deafened, exhaustion, frightened, grappled, prone, restrained

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft. (blind beyond this radius), passive Perception 9

Languages: 

None

Challenge

9 (5,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Reliquary Digestion. When the ooze is in contact with a reliquary or similar consecrated object, the ooze dissolves the object over the course of 1 minute, gaining temporary hit points and leaving behind only a sour, blackened smear.
Adhesive. The ooze adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the ooze is also grappled by it (escape DC 15). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have disadvantage.
Amorphous. The ooze can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing.
Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the ooze or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) acid damage.
Spider Climb. The ooze can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: The ooze makes two Pseudopod attacks.
Pseudopod: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 11 (2d10) acid damage.
```

[concept] identity: The reliquary-ooze seeps up from beneath cathedral floors as a slow tide of viscous rot, flowing around and over consecrated vessels until its acid eats through gilded casing and it can press the holy bones inside into its own dissolving mass. Every round it reaches further toward the richest reliquaries, spreading itself thin across the vault floor to engulf another shrine rather than retreating, and it does not distinguish between bearer and casket — anything that will dissolve is taken into the body. It is patient and reflexive, never fleeing, splitting around cold iron or fire but always reforming toward the altar-hoard; the only thing that makes it recoil is a reliquary it cannot yet digest, and then it simply waits, engulfing the bearer instead.
[concept] lore: Legends say a cathedral whose relics were never translated grew a hunger of its own under the flagstones, and now the vault itself comes up to swallow every saint-box left on the altar.

Ride-By. Krennik ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Mounted Agility. As a bonus action, Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.
Blood-Scent Gallop (1/Day) (Bonus Action). Immediately after dealing damage to a creature that has half of its hit points or fewer, Krennik moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Bearded Axe attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks: one with Bearded Axe and one with Javelin.
Bearded Axe: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) slashing damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) slashing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 6 (1d6 + 3) piercing damage.
Trample Through (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Each creature whose space it enters must succeed on a DC 13 Dexterity saving throw or take 24 (7d6) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone.

Languages None

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Traits

Still Water Veil. The thing can pinpoint the location of contact with its well's water within 60 feet of itself.

Actions

Multiattack. The thing makes two Coils attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Wracking Lash.

Coils. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 12 (2d8 + 3) Bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) Cold damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Wracking Lash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 30 feet that is Grappled by the thing. Failure: 31 (9d6) Cold damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The thing swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the thing, and takes 7 (2d6) Cold damage at the start of each of the thing's turns. The thing can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the thing takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the swallowed creature, the thing must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the thing. If the thing dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

[concept] identity: The thing in the well lunges from the black water as soon as a rope or bucket breaks the surface, seizing ankles and wrists with slick, brine-toughened coils that drag prey down and swallow them whole into its distended, well-bellied maw. Each round it holds what it has grabbed, squeezing and pulling the victim under to drown, and it never emerges fully onto dry ground — it fights from the waterline, retreating down the shaft to swallow when hurt but refusing to abandon the well it claims. It toys with the desperate, bobbing just out of reach and reflexively coiling around anything that probes the dark.
[concept] lore: Something that fell — or was thrown — into a deep well generations ago grew fat on the drowned and the forgotten, and now it wants nothing but the next thing that comes down to drink or draw water.

Senses blindsight 30 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages None

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Sure Footing. Difficult Terrain composed of water, mud, or rubble doesn't cost the riverwight extra movement, and the riverwight can move across water, mud, or rubble without needing to make an ability check.

Actions

Multiattack. The riverwight makes two attacks, using Slam or Flank Clap in any combination.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d8 + 4) Bludgeoning damage plus 6 (1d12) Force damage, and the target has the Prone condition.

Pressure Snap (Recharge 5-6). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the riverwight. Failure: 31 (9d6) Bludgeoning damage. Success: Half damage.

Flank Clap. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d8 + 4) Thunder damage.

Bonus Actions

Whelm. The riverwight whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the riverwight, and takes 6 (1d12) Force damage at the start of each of the riverwight's turns. The riverwight can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the riverwight takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the riverwight must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the riverwight. If the riverwight dies, a whelmed creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

[concept] synthesis text: A mile of river-fed Underdark cave-water given one boneless will, the Pale Undertaker is a sewer-smooth eel of living porcelain that slides up drowned shafts to nose at battlefields, lapping the dead with a scavenger's patience and a crude, nursery-rhyme cunning. It opens a fight by rearing and clapping its flanks, letting a stolen trove of pressure snap outward as a bone-thud of Force that shoves prey off ledges and against stone, then coils and lunges to slam and topple anything that stands its ground. Bloodied, it does not flee and it does not duel — it shrieks a bargain no one agreed to, toys with the weak, and hauls the wounded toward deep water to save for later. It is crudely sapient (INT 6-8), and it is a scavenger drawn to carrion and ruin.

Mimicry. The air elemental can mimic the voices of the drowned. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 13 Wisdom (Insight) check.

Air Form. The air elemental can enter a creature's space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so.

Actions

Multiattack. The air elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Stolen Breath.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the air elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the air elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Huge monstrosity, unaligned
Armor Class

15 (natural armor)

184 (16d12 + 80)

40 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


20 (+5)

blinded, charmed, deafened, frightened, stunned, unconscious

8 (3,900 XP)

+3

Wound Smoke. Any creature that starts its turn within 10 feet of the hydra must succeed on a DC 16 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the end of its next turn. On a successful save, the target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.
Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage.

[concept] identity: The lashed-timber golem opens a fight at a dead run, shouldering through the nearest wall to crush whatever stands behind it, then wades forward battering anything in reach with its squared-off ramming shoulder and splintering fists. Every round it picks the straightest line to its target and takes it, letting load-bearing masonry and ranks of defenders alike break against its frame. It does not negotiate or withdraw — reduced to kindling, it drags itself on and keeps swinging until its lashings give out.
[concept] lore: A siege master's answer to starving castles, this engine of bound beams was built to walk through the walls other armies stop at, and it still seeks the next wall standing.

Stone Golem

Large Construct, Unaligned

AC 18

Speed 30 ft.

Skills Athletics +10, Perception +4

Immunities Poison, Psychic; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 14

Languages Understands Common plus two other languages but can't speak

CR 10 (XP 5,900; PB +4)

Traits

Relentless Pursuit. While the golem is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, the golem's Speed can't be reduced and Difficult Terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.

Immutable Form. The golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The golem makes two attacks, using Slam or Force Bolt in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Crushing Shove.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 9 (2d8) Force damage.

Force Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +9, range 120 ft. 22 (4d10) Force damage.

Crushing Shove. Strength Saving Throw: DC 17, one creature within 5 feet. Failure: The target is pushed up to 15 feet away from the golem and has the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Slow (Recharge 5-6). The golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17).

[concept] identity: The Weeping Saint opens by simply walking forward — a carved stone figure whose slow, unbroken advance lets it break walls and shield-lines alike before its fists ever land. Each round it strikes with grief-heavy stone hands, and the tears that seep from its carved eyes brighten into a holy glow that pours out of every fresh crack it takes. It never flees and never hurries: it bears down on whatever wounded it, weeping all the while, and grimly teaches the lesson that patience is a weapon.
[concept] lore: Carved by a penniless order as a saint who mourns the world's sins, the statue was animated by the depth of that collective grief and now walks the roads looking for a wound worth weeping over — and a congregation worth crushing into the ground.

Incorporeal Movement. The drowned choir can move through other creatures and objects as if they were difficult terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.
Sunlight Sensitivity. While in sunlight, the drowned choir has disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls.

Actions
Multiattack: The drowned choir makes two Drowning Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Wailing Radiance.
Drowning Touch: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 30 (6d8 + 3) necrotic damage.
Wailing Radiance: Each creature in a 30-foot cone must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the end of the drowned choir's next turn.

[concept] identity: The drowned choir opens a fight by singing its single terrible chord, a dirge that rolls through stone and water to pull the warmth from every living throat in earshot. Every round after, it drifts through the flooded nave in slowing, layered harmonies — verse after verse of draining hymn — repositioning through walls to keep every listener inside the sound, so that fleeing only means hearing it longer. Its temperament is patient and inexorable as the tide: it will not retreat, but neither does it rush, letting the song do the killing while it waits for the last voice to fall silent.
[concept] lore: When the cathedral of Saint Vell drowned beneath the reservoir, its choir never stopped singing, and now the whole congregation of the dead shares one throat, one hymn, and one hunger for the breath of the living.

Initiative +2 (12)

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Bloodied Burst. The first time the plague archon is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the plague archon. Failure: 31 (9d6) Poison damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the plague archon, the plague archon and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the plague archon doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The plague archon makes three attacks, using Forelimb or Swarm Bite in any combination.

Forelimb. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 18 (3d8 + 5) Slashing damage plus 13 (3d8) Poison damage.

Swarm Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 29 (7d6 + 5) Piercing damage.

Bonus Actions

Quarry Command. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 17, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: Until the start of the plague archon's next turn, the target has Disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than the plague archon.

[concept] identity: The Plague Archon opens a fight by loosing a shrieking column of its own fly-wracked body, a living swarm-shell that blots sight and scours flesh before it ever closes to claw. Every round it parries with a hardened chitin forelimb and barks chittering commands that bind the lesser swarms to a single target, driving them as a living weapon while it keeps its own wounds regrowing under the crawling mass. Under pressure it does not flee — it folds the swarm inward as a chitinous bulwark, sacrifices drone after drone to blunt a killing stroke, and only breaks if its living armor is stripped away entirely.
[concept] lore: When a blessed warden of the plague-god dies mid-ritual, the flies drawn to its sanctified rot fuse into a chitinous archon that raises the surrounding swarm as its congregation and seeks to spread the contagion it was meant to ward against.

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Gut-Forged Shrapnel. The first time the worm is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the worm. Failure: 42 (12d6) Acid damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Tunneler. The worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Swallow.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Swallow. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature within 10 feet that the worm hit with a melee attack this turn. Failure: The target is swallowed. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the worm, and takes 21 (6d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the worm's turns. If the worm takes 40 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, it must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 10 feet of the worm and has the Prone condition. If the worm dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The Purple Worm that swallowed a war-band's armoury opens by erupting from below, its vast maw clamping down on the nearest foe and then convulsing to drag them whole into a gullet packed with splintered pikes, sword-points, and jammed armour plates that scrape and stab all the way down. Every round after that it burrows in short, grinding surges to re-strike and re-swallow, its gut acting as an armoured second mouth that chews the interior of anything it has taken, and as pressure mounts it simply keeps everything it has caught — grinding, coiling, and crushing rather than ever letting a meal escape. It is not merely a predator but a moving hoard of war-steel, fighting like a living siege-engine with no retreat in it.
[concept] lore: A war-band's slaughtered company and all its blades were swallowed, and the worm's gut has never stopped digesting the iron — it roams the old battlefields, a walking arsenal seeking more steel to feast on.

```
Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

	

25 (+7)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

	

23 (+6)

Skills: 

Perception +13, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Keen Hearing. The red dragon can pinpoint the location of the sound of moving creatures within 120 feet of itself.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Sight Weakness. The red dragon has disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

- Actions/Spellcasting — print's own row
- Actions/Fire Breath — print's own row
- Legendary Actions/Legendary Action Uses: 3 — the chassis
- Legendary Actions/Commanding Presence — print's own row
- Legendary Actions/Fiery Rays — print's own row
- Legendary Actions/Pounce — print's own row

[concept] identity: An old adult red dragon that has learned to favor its sighted side, circling all prey and fights to keep foes in the one arc of clear vision its ruined eye left it. It opens with a low strafing pass and a fan of breath that forces the enemy to shift toward its blind flank, then lands to maul and pin whatever tries to exploit the damaged socket. Wounded and half-blind, it does not flee; it deliberately kills the one creature that noticed the flaw, then turns to grind the rest down with contemptuous patience.
[concept] lore: It survived a knight's lance through the eye and has spent the decades since making the road that took its sight into a toll of bones and ash, hunting anything that enters its valley and remembering which shape of steel maimed it.

Pit Fiend

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 337 (27d10 + 189)

Speed 30 ft., Fly 60 ft. (hover)

MOD
SAVE
STR
26
+8
+8

+12

+2
+2

+13

24
+7
+7

Skills Perception +10, Persuasion +19

Resistances Cold

Immunities Fire, Poison; Poisoned

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Oathbound Chains or (B) Spellcasting to cast Bestow Curse.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Spellcasting. The pit fiend casts one of the following spells, requiring no Material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20):

- At Will: Bestow Curse, Suggestion, Hold Person
- 1/Day Each: Wall of Fire (level 5 version)

Oathbound Chains. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Restrained condition until the end of its next turn.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

[concept] identity: A pit fiend wreathed in molten iron and grave-ash, it opens by raining binding curses down on the living knights around it, anchoring the dying to the spot where their vows break. Each round it harvests an oath — stripping a reaction, a movement, an action from a foe whose word it has caught — and turns that stolen promise into a cage of hot chain and cinder. Under pressure it neither flees nor rushes; it circles, patient and contemptuous, adding fresh clauses to its collection until every blade in the room is still and silent.
[concept] lore: Forged in the deep furnaces where broken pacts are smelted down, a pit fiend descends to battlefields to collect the last words of dying knights, hoarding each oath as a link in the chain it wears and one day intends to hang the world from.

## Ossuar the Bone Sun — in the run

**Concept:** an archlich enthroned on a sun of bone

- clean
- read: def 19 / off 20 → 19.5; sustained 126 (3 × Eldritch Burst + legendary Deathly Teleport); nova none; dmg 126

```
Ossuar the Bone Sun
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If Ossuar fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, Ossuar reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within Ossuar's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ossuar is hit by an attack, it casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ossuar makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) or (B) Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed until the start of Ossuar's next turn.
Spellcasting: Ossuar casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

Ossuar can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. Ossuar regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: Ossuar teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of Ossuar must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Ossuar can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: Ossuar casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. Ossuar can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (Natural Armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CON +16, WIS +10, CHA +16

Damage Immunities: 

Fire

Senses: 

Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.
```

[concept] identity: Vashka the Red anchors himself at the center of a locked shield wall, lacquered plate gleaming, and lets the enemy come to him — the first rank of hobgoblins braces on his barked command and the whole line advances one grinding step at a time, shields never parting. Every round he holds the wall, punishing anyone who tries to slip past a flank with a shoulder-check and an axe stroke, and he never leaves the formation even when the fight turns; when bloodied he tightens ranks and grinds the enemy down rather than fleeing. His temperament is cold, patient, and murderously disciplined — he retreats only in good order, and only to a better line.
[concept] lore: Vashka the Red rose from a legion's shield-bearer to warlord by never losing a rank, and now he sells the discipline of his wall to whichever hobgoblin host pays — but his true desire is to break a rival warlord's line in open field with his own hands.

Cunning-Woman of the Peat

Medium Humanoid, Neutral

AC 15
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 81 (18d8)

Speed 30 ft., Swim 30 ft.

Skills Arcana +6, History +6, Perception +4

Senses Passive Perception 14

Languages Common and any three languages, Sylvan

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. The cunning-woman makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Trespasser's Curse.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. The cunning-woman casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Name-Binding Hex (2/Day). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Incapacitated condition and its Speed is 0 until the end of its next turn.

Trespasser's Curse. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: 19 (3d12) Necrotic damage, and the target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). The cunning-woman casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The cunning-woman casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

[concept] identity: The marsh witch opens a fight from the reeds at range, laying a hex-curl on the nearest trespasser's legs and then layering binding wounds that steal its victims' turns before any blade clears the water. Each round she trades one enemy's action for one crack of her own, threading curses through the muck-bearing air while she circles out of reach, and she does not brawl — she kites, drags, and hobbles the party until the swamp finishes what she started. When bloodied she does not flee honorably; she sinks into the peat, leaves a decoy lamplight glowing behind, and pours every remaining hex into escaping clean.
[concept] lore: A village cunning-woman who bound her own name into the marsh peat to keep the fen from drowning her, she now tolls trespassers for the debt the wetlands are owed.

Actions

Shield Bash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 5 feet that the templar can see. Failure: 9 (2d4 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Medium humanoid, neutral

17

170 (31d8 + 31)

30 ft.

10 (+0)

+4

Magic Resistance. Ilvareth has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.
Misty Step (3/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth casts misty step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, Ilvareth casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ilvareth makes four Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Duskblind.
Spellcasting: Ilvareth casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17):

At will:: detect magic, detect thoughts, disguise self, invisibility, light, mage armor, mage hand, prestidigitation
2/day each:: fly, lightning bolt
1/day each:: cone of cold, mind blank, scrying, teleport
Duskblind: One creature within 60 feet of Ilvareth must succeed on a DC 17 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the end of Ilvareth's next turn.
Arcane Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 150 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (4d10 + 5) force damage.

[concept] identity: Ilvareth Dusksong opens by folding the battlefield in shadow — stepping through darkness to open ground, then hurling bolts of cold force from behind a screen of dimmed light. Every round after, he blinks between shadow-pockets to keep line of sight broken and foes at range, layering controlling shadow-magic over his blasts to pin the reckless in place. If pressed and bloodied, he does not fight to the death: he slips through a final long step of darkness to regroup, and returns only when the shadows favor him again.
[concept] lore: A former court archmage who traded his name to the shadow between places, Ilvareth walks the seams of the world seeking a doorway back to the elf-realm he lost and the power to make it open.

```
Still-Water Monk
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

Hit Points: 

94 (21d8)

Speed: 

30 ft., climb 30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common plus one other language, Draconic

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Hold Breath. The still-water monk can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the still-water monk extra movement, and the still-water monk can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the still-water monk takes the Dodge action.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the still-water monk takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.
Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the still-water monk misses it with a melee attack, the still-water monk makes one Unarmed Strike attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: The still-water monk makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 24 (6d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the still-water monk's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the still-water monk's next turn.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.
```

[concept] identity: The lizardfolk monk fights as a lesson in patience, circling just outside reach with its tail counterbalancing every step until a foe's guard slips, then exploding inward with scaled fists and snapping kicks before withdrawing beyond retaliation. Every round it feints one way and strikes another, using its cold-blooded calm to bait overextension; wounded, it does not flee but slows, reading the fight the way it reads a river current, and only a broken body or an offer of truce ends the duel.
[concept] lore: Hatched in the shallows of a blackwater marsh and drilled in a monastery built on stilts above it, this monk roams the dry lands seeking worthy opponents to test the discipline its order calls 'the still water that carves the canyon.'

## Meshrin — in the run

**Concept:** name: Meshrin | Large lizard folk, 30 feet of movement, Wields a greataxe two-handed and a javelin for the throw. He is a barbarian who rages. Give him a frightening roar. AC 16, 120 hit points.

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Meshrin | 8 (2014) | skirmisher-8-hurler | ok | ok | SOFT: lint:hp-formula | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok |

- structure · soft · lint:hp-formula: HP listed 120 but formula 14d10 + 42 averages 119
- **dropped clause:** "Give him a frightening roar"
- read: def 5 / off 11 → 8; sustained 54 (3 × Javelin); nova Skull-Shove 49; dmg 69

```
Meshrin
Large humanoid, chaotic neutral
Armor Class

16 (breastplate)

Hit Points: 

120 (14d10 + 42)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

14 (+2)

	

10 (+0)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +7

Saving Throws: 

DEX +7, WIS +5

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its Greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Fleet Agility. As a bonus action, Meshrin takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin. It can replace one attack with a use of Triumphant Roar.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d12 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 18 (4d6 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, its javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp, and the target's speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of Meshrin's next turn.
Skull-Shove (Recharge 5-6): Meshrin hurls its javelin at a point it can see within 90 feet. Each creature in a 5-foot-radius sphere centered on that point must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw, taking 49 (14d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, if the target is Large or smaller, it is also knocked prone.
Triumphant Roar: Each creature of Meshrin's choice within 30 feet of it that can see or hear it must make a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw. On a failed save, the target is frightened of Meshrin until the end of its next turn. On a successful save, the target is immune to this creature's terror for 24 hours.
```

- Actions/Javelin — authored for this sheet; a named part; a declared feature; the GM's clause "a javelin for the throw"; weapon javelin
- Actions/Skull-Shove — the chassis
- Actions/Triumphant Roar — template terror-pulse; library draw (seed unstamped); the GM's clause "Give him a frightening roar"; binds the GM's words: roar
- Bonus Actions/Fleet Agility — the chassis

[concept] identity: A towering lizardfolk berserker who opens every fight by hurling a javelin as he charges, then closes the gap and commits to the greataxe — two-handed, swinging wide, feeding his rage with every landed blow. He is a scarred, blood-warm hunter who treats combat as a contest he intends to win.
[concept] lore: Meshrin is a scarred champion of the marsh clans who roamed beyond his swamp to prove his strength, and now he sells that strength to whoever will give him a worthy fight.

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Sockets Locked. While the duellist is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, the duellist's Speed can't be reduced and Difficult Terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.

Great Weapon Master. When the duellist hits a creature with its Greatsword, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The duellist makes two attacks, using Greatsword or Hurled Debris in any combination.

Greatsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 14 (3d6 + 4) Slashing damage plus 7 (2d6) Necrotic damage. If the duellist moved at least 20 feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit and the target is Large or smaller, the target has the Prone condition, and the duellist can make one Greatsword attack against it as a Bonus Action.

Hurled Debris. Ranged Attack Roll: +7, range 60/240 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hired Handaxe | 5 (2014) | skirmisher-8-hurler | ok | ok | ok | SOFT: ko-at-will | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok |

- gates · soft · ko-at-will: Actions/Shortsword: Stunned at will, single target with a repeat save or a one-turn cap (the Ghoul's shape)

```
Hired Handaxe
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15 (chain shirt)

Hit Points: 

97 (13d8 + 39)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

14 (+2)

	

10 (+0)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +5

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Blood Frenzy. The handaxe has advantage on attack rolls against any creature that doesn't have all its hit points.
Battlefield Drift. The handaxe ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Fleet Agility. As a bonus action, the handaxe takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: The handaxe makes three attacks, using Handaxe or Shortsword.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) slashing damage, and the handaxe has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 6 (1d6 + 3) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a handaxe returns to the handaxe's grasp, and the handaxe has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn. Once on each of the handaxe's turns when it hits with this attack, the target must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the handaxe's next turn.
Axe Volley (Recharge 5-6): The handaxe hurls a handaxe at a point it can see within 90 feet. Each creature in a 5-foot-radius sphere centered on that point must make a DC 15 Dexterity saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) slashing damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone if it is Large or smaller.
```

```
Brother Halric
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

13 (chain shirt)

Hit Points: 

38 (7d8 + 7)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

13 (+1)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

Skills: 

Medicine +7, Perception +5, Religion +5

Saving Throws: 

CON +3, WIS +5

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common plus one other language

Challenge

3 (700 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Divine Aid (3/Day). As a bonus action, Halric casts bless, dispel magic, healing word, or lesser restoration, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Actions
Multiattack: Halric makes two attacks: one with Mace and one with Radiant Flame. It can replace one attack with a use of Rallying Shout.
Mace: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 6 (1d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 5 (2d4) radiant damage.
Radiant Flame: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d10) radiant damage.
Rallying Shout: Each creature of Halric's choice within 30 feet of him must succeed on a DC 13 Wisdom saving throw or immediately move up to half its speed in a direction of Halric's choice, using the safest available route.
Spellcasting: Halric casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 13): -

At will: light, thaumaturgy

1/day each: spirit guardians
```

- Bonus Actions/Divine Aid — print's own row

[concept] identity: Brother Halric plants his iron-shod feet and holds the line, opening with a shouted command that steadies and drives his flock forward before stepping into the front rank himself, mace and battered shield ready. He is zealous and unyielding, fighting to the death for his congregation and never yielding ground while any of his people still stand.
